    5142           0 : static float area_split_factor(bContext *C, sAreaJoinData *jd, const wmEvent *event)
    5143             : {
    5144           0 :   float fac = (jd->split_dir == SCREEN_AXIS_V) ?
    5145           0 :                   float(event->xy[0] - jd->sa1->totrct.xmin) / float(jd->sa1->winx + 1) :
    5146           0 :                   float(event->xy[1] - jd->sa1->totrct.ymin) / float(jd->sa1->winy + 1);
    5147             : 
    5148           0 :   if (event->modifier & KM_CTRL) {
    5149             :     /* Snapping on. */
    5150             : 
    5151             :     /* Find nearest neighboring vertex. */
    5152           0 :     const int axis = (jd->split_dir == SCREEN_AXIS_V) ? 0 : 1;
    5153           0 :     int dist = INT_MAX;
    5154           0 :     int loc = 0;
    5155           0 :     for (const ScrVert &v1 : CTX_wm_screen(C)->vertbase) {
    5156           0 :       const int v_loc = (&v1.vec.x)[axis];
    5157           0 :       const int v_dist = abs(v_loc - event->xy[axis]);
    5158           0 :       if (v_dist < dist) {
    5159           0 :         loc = v_loc;
    5160           0 :         dist = v_dist;
    5161             :       }
    5162             :     }
    5163           0 :     float near_fac = (axis) ? float(loc - jd->sa1->totrct.ymin) / float(jd->sa1->winy + 1) :
    5164           0 :                               float(loc - jd->sa1->totrct.xmin) / float(jd->sa1->winx + 1);
    5165             : 
    5166             :     /* Rounded to nearest 12th. */
    5167           0 :     float frac_fac = round(fac * 12.0f) / 12.0f;
    5168             : 
    5169             :     /* Use nearest neighbor or fractional, whichever is closest. */
    5170           0 :     fac = (fabs(near_fac - fac) < fabs(frac_fac - fac)) ? near_fac : frac_fac;
    5171             :   }
    5172             :   else {
    5173             :     /* Slight snap to center when no modifiers are held. */
    5174           0 :     if (fac >= 0.48f && fac < 0.5f) {
    5175           0 :       fac = 0.499999f;
    5176             :     }
    5177           0 :     else if (fac >= 0.5f && fac < 0.52f) {
    5178           0 :       fac = 0.500001f;
    5179             :     }
    5180             :   }
    5181             :

    7132             :  *
    7133             :  * Since during playback the "Pause" button takes the place of the "Reverse Playback" and "Normal
    7134             :  * Playback" buttons, and historically these were all three the same operator, Blender would get
    7135             :  * confused. Under certain conditions it would activate the "Normal Playback" button when the mouse
    7136             :  * was over the "Reverse Playback" button. This is resolved by making the 'Pause' button its own
    7137             :  * operator with its own idname.
    7138             :  *
    7139             :  * Additionally, a fake mouse event is sent to force Blender to reevaluate which button is being
    7140             :  * hovered. This only works when there's a difference in the hovered operator's idname though.
